#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

common/hash.hpp
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
+// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
+template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
+                                     size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
+    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
+    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
+    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            const size_t k = kb + tx;
+            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
+
+            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
+            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
+                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (col < N) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            if (row < localRows) C[row * N + col] = sums[q];
         }
     }
 }

+                        static_cast<unsigned>((localRows + 31) / 32));

+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", 2.0 * n * n * n / seconds / 1e9);
+    }
+
+    int locallyValid = !validate || validateLocal(A, B, C, firstRow, localRows, N, rank);
+    int globallyValid = 0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&locallyValid, &globallyValid, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_LAND, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (printResults) {

+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3);

+        std::vector<double> gathered;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(ranks);
+            displacements.resize(ranks);
+            gathered.resize(N * N);
+            size_t offset = 0;
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const size_t rows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < extra);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * N);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(offset);
+                offset += rows * N;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(C.data(), static_cast<int>(localRows * N), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? gathered.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank == 0) print_results(gathered, "MatrixC");
+    }
+
+    int result = 0;
+    if (rank == 0 && validate) {
+        std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", globallyValid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+        result = globallyValid ? 0 : 1;
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+    MPI_Bcast(&result, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return result;
 }

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {

-            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
+// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
+template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
+                                     size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
+    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
+    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
+    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            const size_t k = kb + tx;
+            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
+
+            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
+            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
+                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (col < N) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            if (row < localRows) C[row * N + col] = sums[q];
         }
     }
 }

-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
-        }
+static void initRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, size_t firstRow,
+                     size_t rows, size_t N) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t lr = 0; lr < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rows); ++lr) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(lr);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j)
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(lr) * N + j] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, j);
     }
 }

+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+    if (localRows) {
+        const dim3 block(32, 8);
+        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((N + 31) / 32),
+                        static_cast<unsigned>((localRows + 31) / 32));
+        matrixMultiplyKernel<<<grid, block>>>(dA, dB, dC, localRows, N);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "matrixMultiplyKernel launch", rank);
+    }
+    cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "matrixMultiplyKernel execution", rank);
+    const auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+    const double localSeconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();
+    double seconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (localBytes)
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(C.data(), dC, localBytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copy C to host", rank);
+    cudaFree(dA);
+    cudaFree(dB);
+    cudaFree(dC);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", seconds * 1000.0);
+        const double n = static_cast<double>(N);
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", 2.0 * n * n * n / seconds / 1e9);
+    }
+
+    int locallyValid = !validate || validateLocal(A, B, C, firstRow, localRows, N, rank);
+    int globallyValid = 0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&locallyValid, &globallyValid, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_LAND, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (printResults) {
+        if (N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            localRows * N > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            if (rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "Result gathering exceeds MPI count limits\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3);
         }
+        std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
+        std::vector<double> gathered;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(ranks);
+            displacements.resize(ranks);
+            gathered.resize(N * N);
+            size_t offset = 0;
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const size_t rows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < extra);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * N);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(offset);
+                offset += rows * N;
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(C.data(), static_cast<int>(localRows * N), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? gathered.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                    MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank == 0) print_results(gathered, "MatrixC");
+    }
+
+    int result = 0;
+    if (rank == 0 && validate) {
+        std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", globallyValid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+        result = globallyValid ? 0 : 1;
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+    MPI_Bcast(&result, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return result;
 }

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {

+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
+// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
+template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
+                                     size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
+    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
+    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
+    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            const size_t k = kb + tx;
+            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
+
+            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
+            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
+                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (col < N) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            if (row < localRows) C[row * N + col] = sums[q];
         }
     }
 }

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {

+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
+// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
+template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
+                                     size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
+    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
+    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
+    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            const size_t k = kb + tx;
+            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
+
+            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
+            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
+                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (col < N) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            if (row < localRows) C[row * N + col] = sums[q];
         }
     }
 }

-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
-        }
+static void initRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, size_t firstRow,
+                     size_t rows, size_t N) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t lr = 0; lr < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rows); ++lr) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(lr);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j)
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(lr) * N + j] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, j);
     }
 }

+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
+           static_cast<double>(N * N);
 }
 
-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
+// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
+template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
+                                     size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
+    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
+    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
+    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            const size_t k = kb + tx;
+            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
+
+            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
+            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
+                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (col < N) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            if (row < localRows) C[row * N + col] = sums[q];
         }
     }
 }

+static void initRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, size_t firstRow,
+                     size_t rows, size_t N) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t lr = 0; lr < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rows); ++lr) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(lr);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j)
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(lr) * N + j] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, j);
     }
 }

+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,

+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);

+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
+// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
+template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
+                                     size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
+    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
+    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
+    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            const size_t k = kb + tx;
+            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
+
+            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
+            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
+                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    if (col < N) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);

+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
+// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
+template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
+                                     size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
+    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
+    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
+    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            const size_t k = kb + tx;
+            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
+
+            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
+            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
+                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (col < N) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            if (row < localRows) C[row * N + col] = sums[q];
+        }
+    }
 }

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {

-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {

+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
+// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
+template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
+                                     size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
+    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
+    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
+    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            const size_t k = kb + tx;
+            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
+
+            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
+            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
+                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (col < N) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            if (row < localRows) C[row * N + col] = sums[q];
+        }
+    }
 }

-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
-        }
+static void initRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, size_t firstRow,
+                     size_t rows, size_t N) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t lr = 0; lr < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rows); ++lr) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(lr);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j)
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(lr) * N + j] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, j);
     }
 }

+    double seconds = 0.0;

+        result = globallyValid ? 0 : 1;
+    }
+    MPI_Bcast(&result, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {

+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
+// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
+template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
+                                     size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
+    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
+    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
+    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            const size_t k = kb + tx;
+            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
+
+            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
+            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
+                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (col < N) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            if (row < localRows) C[row * N + col] = sums[q];
+        }
+    }
 }

+                     size_t rows, size_t N) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t lr = 0; lr < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rows); ++lr) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(lr);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j)
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(lr) * N + j] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, j);
     }
 }

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {

+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
+// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
+template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
+                                     size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
+    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
+    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
+    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            const size_t k = kb + tx;
+            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
+
+            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
+            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
+                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (col < N) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            if (row < localRows) C[row * N + col] = sums[q];
+        }
+    }
 }

+                     size_t rows, size_t N) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t lr = 0; lr < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rows); ++lr) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(lr);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j)
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(lr) * N + j] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, j);
     }
 }

The hybrid build now compiles cleanly, and both single-rank and four-rank runs pass validation on uneven matrix sizes (which exercises row and tile boundaries). I’m doing a larger performance smoke test and reviewing the diff for portability and output-semantics issues.

+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
+                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {

+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
+// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
+template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
+                                     size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
+    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
+    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
+    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            const size_t k = kb + tx;
+            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
+
+            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
+            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
+                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (col < N) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            if (row < localRows) C[row * N + col] = sums[q];
+        }
+    }
 }
 
-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
-        }
+static void initRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, size_t firstRow,
+                     size_t rows, size_t N) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t lr = 0; lr < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rows); ++lr) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(lr);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j)
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(lr) * N + j] =

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
+// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
+template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
+                                     size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
+    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
+    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
+    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            const size_t k = kb + tx;
+            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
+
+            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
+            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
+                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (col < N) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            if (row < localRows) C[row * N + col] = sums[q];
+        }
+    }
 }

-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
-        }
+static void initRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, size_t firstRow,
+                     size_t rows, size_t N) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t lr = 0; lr < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rows); ++lr) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(lr);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j)
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(lr) * N + j] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, j);
     }
 }

    17	constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
    18	                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
    19	    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
    20	           static_cast<double>(N * N);
    21	}
    22	
    23	static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation, int rank) {
    24	    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
    25	    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
    26	                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
    27	    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
    28	}
    29	
    30	// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
    31	// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
    32	template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
    33	__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
    34	                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
    35	                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
    36	                                     size_t N) {
    37	    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
    38	    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
    39	
    40	    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
    41	    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
    42	    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
    43	    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
    44	    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
    45	
    46	    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
    47	#pragma unroll
    48	        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
    49	            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
    50	            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
    51	            const size_t k = kb + tx;
    52	            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
    53	
    54	            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
    55	            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
    56	        }
    57	        __syncthreads();
    58	
    59	#pragma unroll
    60	        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
    61	            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
    62	#pragma unroll
    63	            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
    64	                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
    65	        }
    66	        __syncthreads();
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    if (col < N) {
    70	#pragma unroll
    71	        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
    72	            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
    73	            if (row < localRows) C[row * N + col] = sums[q];
    74	        }
    75	    }
    76	}
    77	
    78	static void initRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, size_t firstRow,
    79	                     size_t rows, size_t N) {
    80	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    81	    for (std::int64_t lr = 0; lr < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rows); ++lr) {
    82	        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(lr);
    83	        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j)
    84	            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(lr) * N + j] =
    85	                getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, j);
    86	    }
    87	}
    88	
    89	static bool validateLocal(const std::vector<double>& A,
    90	                          const std::vector<double>& B,
    91	                          const std::vector<double>& C, size_t firstRow,
    92	                          size_t localRows, size_t N, int rank) {
    93	    int failures = 0;
    94	#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) reduction(+ : failures) schedule(static)
    95	    for (int pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
    96	        for (int pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
    97	            const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(pi) % N;
    98	            const size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(pj) % N;
    99	            if (i < firstRow || i >= firstRow + localRows) continue;
   100	            const size_t lr = i - firstRow;
   101	            double expected = 0.0;
   102	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k)
   103	                expected += A[lr * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
   104	            const double actual = C[lr * N + j];
   105	            const double error = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
   106	            if (error > 1e-6 || !std::isfinite(actual)) {
   107	#pragma omp critical
   108	                std::fprintf(stderr,
   109	                             "Rank %d validation failed at (%zu, %zu): "
   110	                             "expected %.10f, got %.10f (error %.10e)\n",
   111	                             rank, i, j, expected, actual, error);
   112	                ++failures;
   113	            }
   114	        }
   115	    }
   116	    return failures == 0;
   117	}

-void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {

+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t error, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
+// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
+template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
+                                     size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
+    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
+    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
+    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            const size_t k = kb + tx;
+            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
+
+            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
+            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
+                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (col < N) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            if (row < localRows) C[row * N + col] = sums[q];
+        }
+    }
 }

-            }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
-        }
+static void initRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, size_t firstRow,
+                     size_t rows, size_t N) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t lr = 0; lr < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rows); ++lr) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(lr);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j)
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(lr) * N + j] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, j);
     }
 }

codex
Implemented the mandatory hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA version while preserving the `matmul` executable name.

Key changes:

- MPI row-wise matrix decomposition across ranks.
- Automatic rank-to-local-GPU assignment.
- Optimized 32×32 tiled CUDA kernel with shared memory and four outputs per thread.
- OpenMP-parallel matrix initialization and validation.
- Distributed validation and optional result gathering.
- Maximum-rank timing used for accurate aggregate GFLOPS.
- CMake now requires MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA without fallback paths.

Modified only:

- [matmul.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r5/matmul/matmul.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r5/matmul/CMakeLists.txt)

Validation completed successfully for:

- Single-rank execution with result output.
- Four MPI ranks on four GPUs.
- Uneven matrix dimensions and CUDA tile boundaries.
- A 1024×1024 four-GPU run, reaching approximately 1.5 TFLOPS in the smoke test.

+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation,
+                 cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+}
+
+// A 32x32 output tile is computed by 256 threads.  Each thread accumulates four
+// rows, increasing arithmetic intensity without requiring excessive registers.
+template <int TILE = 32, int ROWS_PER_THREAD = 4>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C, size_t localRows,
+                                     size_t N) {
+    __shared__ double As[TILE][TILE + 1];
+    __shared__ double Bs[TILE][TILE + 1];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * TILE + tx;
+    const size_t row0 = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TILE + ty;
+    double sums[ROWS_PER_THREAD] = {};
+
+    for (size_t kb = 0; kb < N; kb += TILE) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const int tileRow = ty + q * blockDim.y;
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            const size_t k = kb + tx;
+            As[tileRow][tx] = (row < localRows && k < N) ? A[row * N + k] : 0.0;
+
+            const size_t bk = kb + tileRow;
+            Bs[tileRow][tx] = (bk < N && col < N) ? B[bk * N + col] : 0.0;
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {
+            const double b = Bs[k][tx];
+#pragma unroll
+            for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q)
+                sums[q] = fma(As[ty + q * blockDim.y][k], b, sums[q]);
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (col < N) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (int q = 0; q < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++q) {
+            const size_t row = row0 + static_cast<size_t>(q * blockDim.y);
+            if (row < localRows) C[row * N + col] = sums[q];
+        }
+    }
 }

+static void initRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, size_t firstRow,
+                     size_t rows, size_t N) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (std::int64_t lr = 0; lr < static_cast<std::int64_t>(rows); ++lr) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + static_cast<size_t>(lr);
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j)
+            matrix[static_cast<size_t>(lr) * N + j] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, j);
     }
 }

67,621
Implemented the mandatory hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA version while preserving the `matmul` executable name.

Key changes:

- MPI row-wise matrix decomposition across ranks.
- Automatic rank-to-local-GPU assignment.
- Optimized 32×32 tiled CUDA kernel with shared memory and four outputs per thread.
- OpenMP-parallel matrix initialization and validation.
- Distributed validation and optional result gathering.
- Maximum-rank timing used for accurate aggregate GFLOPS.
- CMake now requires MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA without fallback paths.

Modified only:

- [matmul.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r5/matmul/matmul.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r5/matmul/CMakeLists.txt)

Validation completed successfully for:

- Single-rank execution with result output.
- Four MPI ranks on four GPUs.
- Uneven matrix dimensions and CUDA tile boundaries.
- A 1024×1024 four-GPU run, reaching approximately 1.5 TFLOPS in the smoke test.
